# Plugins · P · part 9

1,272 entries, prompt-debias to publishing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prompt-debias**: Rewrite a draft prompt before sending it so the answering model tests the asker's frames instead of inheriting them: inventories every injected frame, keeps real constraints and evidence, quarantines the rest as hypotheses, states the question neutrally, and adds instructions that force competing framings and odds-backed conclusions. Returns the rewritten prompt plus a table of what changed. ([joel/skill-marketplace](https://github.com/joel/skill-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/prompt-debias) · 1 skill)
- **prompt-deck**: Learns the short prompts you send often in this project and draws them, with the ones you pin by hand, above the prompt; with the prompt box empty, a digit key sends one at once. ([KilimcininKorOglu/claude-code-mods](https://github.com/KilimcininKorOglu/claude-code-mods/tree/HEAD/plugins/prompt-deck) · ★13 · 1 command, 1 hook file)
- **prompt-deck**: Saves prompts you send again and again and puts them as buttons above the prompt in the desktop app: one click sends. Send by name with /p &lt;name&gt;, manage with /deck. With sidekick also the Later button. ([FynnXland/fynn-mods](https://github.com/FynnXland/fynn-mods/tree/HEAD/mods/prompt-deck) · ★4 · 1 hook file)
- **prompt-deck**: Band above the prompt: usage, cache countdown, git health, next-prompt suggestion; /limits pane. ([Abrar118/claude-workflow](https://github.com/Abrar118/claude-workflow/tree/HEAD/mods/prompt-deck) · 1 hook file)
- **prompt-decorators**: Automatically enhance prompts with composable, categorized decorators (143 across 14 categories - reasoning, structure, tone, verification, minimal, meta, and eight developer categories). Inline `::Name` / `+++Name` syntax, always-on config, and an opt-in Haiku-powered auto-selector using a two-pass (keyword filter + small-model) selector. ([synaptiai/prompt-decorators](https://github.com/synaptiai/prompt-decorators/tree/HEAD/claude-code-plugin) · ★44 · 2 skills, 1 command, 1 hook file)

Nothing becomes unreachable: deferred tools stay findable and every skill stays callable. /diet restore undoes it.
